23:T452,Anchor 4: Commonwealth Bank of Australia named H2O AI Cloud in its own announcement of November 2021 and TechCrunch named it as a core product the same month, so the product has been available for more than three years; a regulated customer is named, the vendor publishes release notes about monthly, and its support terms cover the current major release and the one before it for up to twelve months. Anchor 5 is not treated as fully met. It needs bank customers in production confirmed by a source other than the vendor, and the only bank evidence comes from Commonwealth Bank, which led the vendor's 2021 funding round, holds a minority stake and has an executive on the vendor's board, so it is not at arm's length. Its November 2022 page says more than 20 use cases were in production 'or soon to be', and the iTnews report of April 2023 that the bank had deployed models built on the H2O.ai platform relays a bank employee speaking at a vendor webinar and does not name the product. 5b:T452,Anchor 4: Commonwealth Bank of Australia named H2O AI Cloud in its own announcement of November 2021 and TechCrunch named it as a core product the same month, so the product has been available for more than three years; a regulated customer is named, the vendor publishes release notes about monthly, and its support terms cover the current major release and the one before it for up to twelve months. Anchor 5 is not treated as fully met. It needs bank customers in production confirmed by a source other than the vendor, and the only bank evidence comes from Commonwealth Bank, which led the vendor's 2021 funding round, holds a minority stake and has an executive on the vendor's board, so it is not at arm's length. Its November 2022 page says more than 20 use cases were in production 'or soon to be', and the iTnews report of April 2023 that the bank had deployed models built on the H2O.ai platform relays a bank employee speaking at a vendor webinar and does not name the product.
